`crates/store/src/exchange.rs` dosyasını yalnızca test bölümüyle oluştur (5 test: `notes_are_excluded_by_default`, `round_trip_into_empty_store_is_exact_and_idempotent`, `invalid_files_and_records_are_rejected_atomically`, `unreadable_imported_settings_take_current_defaults_with_warning`, `imported_moments_uuids_and_empty_clients_are_checked`):

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::charts::testutil::new_client;
    use crate::db::testutil::store;
    use crate::open::StoreWarning;
    use crate::view_state::ViewState;
    use jyotish_core::{CalcSettings, Graha, NodeKind};
    use serde_json::json;

    fn populated() -> (Store, u32) {
        let mut s = store();
        let (c, ch) = s.create_client(&new_client("Ayşe")).unwrap();
        let settings = CalcSettings {
            node: NodeKind::True,
            ..CalcSettings::default()
        };
        s.save_calc_settings(ch.id, &settings).unwrap();
        s.set_chart_tags(ch.id, &["araştırma".into()]).unwrap();
        s.save_view_state(
            ch.id,
            &ViewState {
                dasha_expanded_path: vec![Graha::Venus],
                ..ViewState::default()
            },
        )
        .unwrap();
        s.add_note(c.id, Some(ch.id), "gizli seans notu").unwrap();
        (s, c.id)
    }

    #[test]
    fn notes_are_excluded_by_default() {
        let (mut s, _) = populated();
        let text = s.export_json(&ExportOptions::default()).unwrap();
        assert!(!text.contains("gizli seans notu"));
        let f: ExportFile = serde_json::from_str(&text).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((f.format.as_str(), f.schema_version), (EXPORT_FORMAT, 1));
        assert_eq!(f.clients[0].notes, None);
        assert_eq!(f.clients[0].charts[0].tags, ["araştırma"]);
        assert_eq!(f.clients[0].charts[0].calc_settings["node"], json!("True"));
        let with = s
            .export_json(&ExportOptions {
                include_notes: true,
                client_ids: None,
            })
            .unwrap();
        assert!(with.contains("gizli seans notu"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn round_trip_into_empty_store_is_exact_and_idempotent() {
        let (mut src, cid) = populated();
        let opts = ExportOptions {
            include_notes: true,
            client_ids: Some(vec![cid]),
        };
        let text = src.export_json(&opts).unwrap();
        let mut dst = store();
        let r = dst.import_json(&text).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            r,
            ImportReport {
                clients_added: 1,
                charts_added: 1,
                notes_added: 1,
                skipped: 0,
                warnings: vec![]
            }
        );
        let a = src.client(cid).unwrap();
        let b = &dst.list_clients(10).unwrap()[0];
        assert_eq!(
            (&a.uuid, &a.name, &a.created_at),
            (&b.uuid, &b.name, &b.created_at)
        );
        let ca = &src.charts_of(a.id).unwrap()[0];
        let cb = &dst.charts_of(b.id).unwrap()[0];
        assert_eq!(
            (&ca.uuid, &ca.birth, &ca.resolved, &ca.tags),
            (&cb.uuid, &cb.birth, &cb.resolved, &cb.tags)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            src.calc_settings(ca.id).unwrap(),
            dst.calc_settings(cb.id).unwrap()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            dst.view_state(cb.id).unwrap().dasha_expanded_path,
            [Graha::Venus]
        );
        assert_eq!(b.last_chart_id, Some(cb.id));
        let na = &src.notes(a.id, 10).unwrap()[0];
        let nb = &dst.notes(b.id, 10).unwrap()[0];
        assert_eq!(
            (&na.uuid, &na.body, nb.chart_id),
            (&nb.uuid, &nb.body, Some(cb.id))
        );
        // İkinci kez: hepsi atlanır, kopya oluşmaz.
        let again = dst.import_json(&text).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            (again.clients_added, again.charts_added, again.skipped),
            (0, 0, 3)
        );
        assert_eq!(dst.list_clients(10).unwrap().len(), 1);
        // Dışa aktarım yeniden üretilebilir (exported_at hariç).
        let mut x: Value = serde_json::from_str(&src.export_json(&opts).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let mut y: Value = serde_json::from_str(&dst.export_json(&opts).unwrap()).unwrap();
        x["exported_at"] = Value::Null;
        y["exported_at"] = Value::Null;
        assert_eq!(x, y);
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_files_and_records_are_rejected_atomically() {
        let mut s = store();
        assert!(matches!(
            s.import_json("nope"),
            Err(StoreError::ImportFormat(_))
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            s.import_json(r#"{"format":"other","schema_version":1}"#),
            Err(StoreError::ImportFormat(_))
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            s.import_json(r#"{"format":"jyotish-desk","schema_version":2,"clients":[]}"#),
            Err(StoreError::ImportVersion {
                found: 2,
                supported: 1
            })
        ));
        let (mut src, _) = populated();
        let mut v: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(&src.export_json(&ExportOptions::default()).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let second = v["clients"][0].clone();
        let mut bad = second.clone();
        bad["uuid"] = json!(Uuid::new_v4().to_string());
        bad["charts"][0]["uuid"] = json!("4b0f2a8e-0000-4000-8000-000000000001");
        bad["charts"][0]["birth"]["date"] = json!("2000-13-01");
        v["clients"] = json!([second, bad]);
        match s.import_json(&v.to_string()) {
            Err(StoreError::ImportRecord { item, field }) => {
                assert_eq!(item, "4b0f2a8e-0000-4000-8000-000000000001");
                assert_eq!(field, "birth.date");
            }
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        }
        // Geri alındı: ilk (geçerli) danışan da yazılmadı.
        assert!(s.list_clients(10).unwrap().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn unreadable_imported_settings_take_current_defaults_with_warning() {
        let (mut src, _) = populated();
        let mut v: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(&src.export_json(&ExportOptions::default()).unwrap()).unwrap();
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["calc_settings"] = json!({"schema_version": 5});
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["view_state"] = json!("garbage");
        let mut dst = store();
        let r = dst.import_json(&v.to_string()).unwrap();
        let uuid = v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["uuid"]
            .as_str()
            .unwrap()
            .to_owned();
        assert_eq!(
            r.warnings,
            [ImportWarning {
                item: uuid,
                code: ImportWarningCode::CalcSettingsReset
            }]
        );
        let c = &dst.list_clients(1).unwrap()[0];
        let ch = &dst.charts_of(c.id).unwrap()[0];
        assert_eq!(
            dst.calc_settings(ch.id).unwrap(),
            SettingsLoad::Current(CalcSettings::default())
        );
        assert!(dst
            .view_state(ch.id)
            .unwrap()
            .dasha_expanded_path
            .is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn imported_moments_uuids_and_empty_clients_are_checked() {
        let (mut src, _) = populated();
        let exported: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(&src.export_json(&ExportOptions::default()).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let rejected = |v: &Value| match store().import_json(&v.to_string()) {
            Err(StoreError::ImportRecord { field, .. }) => field,
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        };
        // Doğum verisiyle uyuşmayan an (aynı tzdb sürümü): sessizce yanlış harita açılmaz.
        let mut v = exported.clone();
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["resolved"]["jd_ut"] = json!(2_451_000.25);
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["resolved"]["utc"] = json!("1998-07-06T18:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(rejected(&v), "resolved");
        let mut v = exported.clone();
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["resolved"]["utc"] = json!("=1+1");
        assert_eq!(rejected(&v), "resolved");
        // Başka tzdb sürümüyle kaydedilmiş IANA haritası: an korunur (karar açılışta), ama
        // `jd_ut` ile `utc` aynı anı göstermelidir.
        let mut v = exported.clone();
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["resolved"]["tzdb_version"] = json!("1999a");
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["resolved"]["utc"] = json!("2000-01-01T11:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(rejected(&v), "resolved");
        v["clients"][0]["charts"][0]["resolved"]["jd_ut"] = json!(2_451_544.958_333_333_5);
        let mut dst = store();
        dst.import_json(&v.to_string()).unwrap();
        let c = &dst.list_clients(1).unwrap()[0];
        let o = dst.open_client(c.id, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(o.chart.resolved.utc, "2000-01-01T11:00:00Z");
        assert!(matches!(o.warnings[..], [StoreWarning::TzdbChanged { .. }]));
        // Haritasız danışan oluşmaz.
        let mut v = exported.clone();
        v["clients"][0]["charts"] = json!([]);
        assert_eq!(rejected(&v), "charts");
        // UUID yazımı (büyük harf) aynı kaydı gösterir: kopya oluşmaz.
        let mut v = exported.clone();
        for p in ["/clients/0/uuid", "/clients/0/charts/0/uuid"] {
            let up = v.pointer(p).unwrap().as_str().unwrap().to_uppercase();
            *v.pointer_mut(p).unwrap() = json!(up);
        }
        let mut dst = store();
        dst.import_json(&exported.to_string()).unwrap();
        let again = dst.import_json(&v.to_string()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            (again.clients_added, again.charts_added, again.skipped),
            (0, 0, 2)
        );
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Uygulamayı yaz**

`crates/store/src/exchange.rs` dosyasının başına, `#[cfg(test)]` satırının **üstüne** ekle:

```rust
//! Sürümlü JSON içe/dışa aktarma (spec §5 "İçe ve dışa aktarma"). Kayıtlar UUID ile
//! birleştirilir: var olan UUID atlanır, üzerine yazılmaz. Notlar yalnızca istenirse
//! dışa aktarılır (varsayılan kapalı; notlar hassas veridir).

use jyotish_core::JD_UT_RANGE;
use rusqlite::{params, Connection, OptionalExtension};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::birth::{parse_birth, resolve_parsed, ParsedBirth};
use crate::charts::{clean_name, insert_chart, insert_client, ChartRow};
use crate::db::format_ts;
use crate::model::{BirthData, ChartKind, Gender, ResolvedRecord, TzChoice};
use crate::settings::{load_calc_value, SettingsLoad};
use crate::view_state::{chart_view_json, parse_dasha_path};
use crate::{Store, StoreError};

pub const EXPORT_FORMAT: &str = "jyotish-desk";
pub const EXPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 1;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "specta", derive(specta::Type))]
pub struct ExportOptions {
    /// "Notları dahil et"; varsayılan kapalı.
    pub include_notes: bool,
    /// Boşsa tüm danışanlar.
    pub client_ids: Option<Vec<u32>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ExportFile {
    pub format: String,
    pub schema_version: u32,
    pub exported_at: String,
    pub clients: Vec<ExportClient>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ExportClient {
    pub uuid: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub gender: Option<Gender>,
    pub created_at: String,
    pub updated_at: String,
    pub charts: Vec<ExportChart>,
    /// Notlar dahil edilmediyse `null`.
    pub notes: Option<Vec<ExportNote>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ExportChart {
    pub uuid: String,
    pub label: String,
    pub kind: ChartKind,
    pub birth: BirthData,
    pub resolved: ResolvedRecord,
    /// Saklanan JSON aynen (daha yeni sürümün alanları korunur).
    pub calc_settings: Value,
    /// Haritanın görünüm kısmı (`dasha_expanded_path`); düzenler kullanıcınındır, aktarılmaz.
    pub view_state: Value,
    pub tags: Vec<String>,
    pub created_at: String,
    pub updated_at: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ExportNote {
    pub uuid: String,
    pub chart_uuid: Option<String>,
    pub body: String,
    pub created_at: String,
    pub updated_at: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "specta", derive(specta::Type))]
pub enum ImportWarningCode {
    /// Haritanın ayarı okunamadı; o anki genel varsayılan yazıldı.
    CalcSettingsReset,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "specta", derive(specta::Type))]
pub struct ImportWarning {
    /// Harita UUID'si ya da JHD dosya adı.
    pub item: String,
    pub code: ImportWarningCode,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "specta", derive(specta::Type))]
pub struct ImportReport {
    pub clients_added: u32,
    pub charts_added: u32,
    pub notes_added: u32,
    /// Zaten var olan (UUID ya da JHD eşleşmesi) ve atlanan kayıtlar.
    pub skipped: u32,
    pub warnings: Vec<ImportWarning>,
}

fn id_by_uuid(conn: &Connection, table: &str, uuid: &str) -> Result<Option<u32>, StoreError> {
    let sql = format!("SELECT id FROM {table} WHERE uuid = ?1");
    Ok(conn
        .query_row(&sql, params![uuid], |r| r.get(0))
        .optional()?)
}

/// UUID'yi denetler ve kanonik biçime (küçük harf, tireli) çevirir; birleştirme bu biçimle
/// yapılır.
fn check_uuid(uuid: &str) -> Result<String, StoreError> {
    Uuid::parse_str(uuid)
        .map(|u| u.to_string())
        .map_err(|_| StoreError::ImportRecord {
            item: uuid.to_owned(),
            field: "uuid",
        })
}

/// İçe alınan çözülmüş anı denetler. Güncel tzdb ile yeniden çözülen an dosyadakiyle aynıysa
/// yeni çözüm yazılır. Farklıysa yalnızca IANA dilimli ve başka bir tzdb sürümüyle
/// kaydedilmiş harita dosyadaki anı korur (karar açılışta kullanıcınındır); o durumda `utc`
/// okunabilir, `jd_ut` aynı anı göstermeli (≤ 1 sn) ve hesap aralığında olmalıdır. Diğer her
/// uyuşmazlık `None` verir: doğum verisiyle uyuşmayan bir anla harita sessizce açılmaz.
fn import_resolved(p: &ParsedBirth, r: &ResolvedRecord) -> Option<ResolvedRecord> {
    let stored: atlas::Timestamp = r.utc.parse().ok()?;
    if let Ok(fresh) = resolve_parsed(p) {
        if fresh.utc.parse::<atlas::Timestamp>().ok() == Some(stored) {
            return Some(fresh);
        }
    }
    let other_tzdb =
        matches!(p.canonical.tz, TzChoice::Iana(_)) && r.tzdb_version != atlas::tzdb_version();
    let jd = stored.as_millisecond() as f64 / 86_400_000.0 + 2_440_587.5;
    if !other_tzdb || !JD_UT_RANGE.contains(&r.jd_ut) || (r.jd_ut - jd).abs() * 86_400.0 > 1.0 {
        return None;
    }
    Some(ResolvedRecord {
        utc: stored.to_string(),
        ..r.clone()
    })
}

/// Geçerli bir `format_ts` zaman damgası değilse `now`.
fn ts_or(ts: &str, now: &str) -> String {
    match ts.parse::<atlas::Timestamp>() {
        Ok(t) => format_ts(t),
        Err(_) => now.to_owned(),
    }
}

impl Store {
    /// Okunabilir (girintili) JSON; danışanlar ve haritalar kimlik sırasında.
    pub fn export_json(&mut self, opts: &ExportOptions) -> Result<String, StoreError> {
        let exported_at = self.now();
        let ids: Vec<u32> = match &opts.client_ids {
            Some(ids) => {
                for id in ids {
                    self.client(*id)?;
                }
                ids.clone()
            }
            None => {
                let mut stmt = self.conn.prepare("SELECT id FROM clients ORDER BY id")?;
                let rows = stmt.query_map([], |r| r.get(0))?;
                rows.collect::<Result<Vec<u32>, _>>()?
            }
        };
        let mut clients = Vec::with_capacity(ids.len());
        for id in ids {
            let c = self.client(id)?;
            let mut charts = self.charts_of(id)?;
            charts.sort_by_key(|ch| ch.id);
            let mut out_charts = Vec::with_capacity(charts.len());
            for ch in &charts {
                let (calc, view): (String, String) = self.conn.query_row(
                    "SELECT calc_settings_json, view_state_json FROM charts WHERE id = ?1",
                    params![ch.id],
                    |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)),
                )?;
                out_charts.push(ExportChart {
                    uuid: ch.uuid.clone(),
                    label: ch.label.clone(),
                    kind: ch.kind,
                    birth: ch.birth.clone(),
                    resolved: ch.resolved.clone(),
                    calc_settings: serde_json::from_str(&calc).unwrap_or(Value::Null),
                    view_state: serde_json::from_str(&view).unwrap_or(Value::Null),
                    tags: ch.tags.clone(),
                    created_at: ch.created_at.clone(),
                    updated_at: ch.updated_at.clone(),
                });
            }
            let notes = if opts.include_notes {
                let mut notes = self.notes(id, u32::MAX)?;
                notes.reverse();
                Some(
                    notes
                        .into_iter()
                        .map(|n| ExportNote {
                            uuid: n.uuid,
                            chart_uuid: n.chart_id.and_then(|cid| {
                                charts
                                    .iter()
                                    .find(|ch| ch.id == cid)
                                    .map(|ch| ch.uuid.clone())
                            }),
                            body: n.body,
                            created_at: n.created_at,
                            updated_at: n.updated_at,
                        })
                        .collect(),
                )
            } else {
                None
            };
            clients.push(ExportClient {
                uuid: c.uuid,
                name: c.name,
                gender: c.gender,
                created_at: c.created_at,
                updated_at: c.updated_at,
                charts: out_charts,
                notes,
            });
        }
        Ok(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&ExportFile {
            format: EXPORT_FORMAT.into(),
            schema_version: EXPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            exported_at,
            clients,
        })?)
    }

    /// Hepsi ya da hiçbiri: geçersiz bir kayıt tüm içe aktarmayı geri alır ve kaydın
    /// UUID'siyle `ImportRecord` döner. Çözülmüş an doğum verisine karşı denetlenir
    /// (`import_resolved`); okunamayan ayar o anki genel varsayılanla değiştirilir. Haritasız
    /// danışan oluşmaz.
    pub fn import_json(&mut self, text: &str) -> Result<ImportReport, StoreError> {
        let value: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| StoreError::ImportFormat(e.to_string()))?;
        if value.get("format").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some(EXPORT_FORMAT) {
            return Err(StoreError::ImportFormat("format".into()));
        }
        let version = value
            .get("schema_version")
            .and_then(Value::as_u64)
            .and_then(|v| u32::try_from(v).ok())
            .ok_or_else(|| StoreError::ImportFormat("schema_version".into()))?;
        if version == 0 || version > EXPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION {
            return Err(StoreError::ImportVersion {
                found: version,
                supported: EXPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            });
        }
        let file: ExportFile =
            serde_json::from_value(value).map_err(|e| StoreError::ImportFormat(e.to_string()))?;
        let defaults = self.app_prefs()?.calc_settings;
        let now = self.now();
        let mut report = ImportReport::default();
        let tx = self.conn.transaction()?;
        for c in &file.clients {
            let client_uuid = check_uuid(&c.uuid)?;
            let client_id = match id_by_uuid(&tx, "clients", &client_uuid)? {
                Some(id) => {
                    report.skipped += 1;
                    id
                }
                None => {
                    let name =
                        clean_name(&c.name, "name").map_err(|_| StoreError::ImportRecord {
                            item: client_uuid.clone(),
                            field: "name",
                        })?;
                    report.clients_added += 1;
                    insert_client(
                        &tx,
                        &client_uuid,
                        &name,
                        c.gender,
                        &ts_or(&c.created_at, &now),
                        &ts_or(&c.updated_at, &now),
                    )?
                }
            };
            let mut first_new_chart = None;
            for ch in &c.charts {
                let chart_uuid = check_uuid(&ch.uuid)?;
                if id_by_uuid(&tx, "charts", &chart_uuid)?.is_some() {
                    report.skipped += 1;
                    continue;
                }
                let record = |field| StoreError::ImportRecord {
                    item: chart_uuid.clone(),
                    field,
                };
                let parsed = parse_birth(&ch.birth).map_err(|e| match e {
                    StoreError::InvalidInput(f) => record(f),
                    _ => record("birth"),
                })?;
                let label = clean_name(&ch.label, "label").map_err(|_| record("label"))?;
                let resolved =
                    import_resolved(&parsed, &ch.resolved).ok_or_else(|| record("resolved"))?;
                let settings = match load_calc_value(ch.calc_settings.clone(), &defaults) {
                    SettingsLoad::Reset { settings, .. } => {
                        report.warnings.push(ImportWarning {
                            item: chart_uuid.clone(),
                            code: ImportWarningCode::CalcSettingsReset,
                        });
                        settings
                    }
                    other => other.settings().clone(),
                };
                let path = parse_dasha_path(ch.view_state.get("dasha_expanded_path"));
                let chart_id = insert_chart(
                    &tx,
                    &ChartRow {
                        uuid: &chart_uuid,
                        client_id,
                        label: &label,
                        kind: ch.kind,
                        birth: &parsed.canonical,
                        resolved: &resolved,
                        calc_json: &serde_json::to_string(&settings)?,
                        view_json: &chart_view_json(&path),
                        created_at: &ts_or(&ch.created_at, &now),
                        updated_at: &ts_or(&ch.updated_at, &now),
                    },
                )?;
                for t in crate::notes::normalize_tags(&ch.tags).map_err(|_| record("tags"))? {
                    tx.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO tags (name) VALUES (?1)", params![t])?;
                    tx.execute(
                        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO chart_tags (chart_id, tag_id)
                         SELECT ?1, id FROM tags WHERE name = ?2",
                        params![chart_id, t],
                    )?;
                }
                first_new_chart.get_or_insert(chart_id);
                report.charts_added += 1;
            }
            if let Some(chart_id) = first_new_chart {
                tx.execute(
                    "UPDATE clients SET last_chart_id = ?1 WHERE id = ?2 AND last_chart_id IS NULL",
                    params![chart_id, client_id],
                )?;
            }
            let has_chart: bool = tx.query_row(
                "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM charts WHERE client_id = ?1)",
                params![client_id],
                |r| r.get(0),
            )?;
            if !has_chart {
                return Err(StoreError::ImportRecord {
                    item: client_uuid,
                    field: "charts",
                });
            }
            for n in c.notes.iter().flatten() {
                let note_uuid = check_uuid(&n.uuid)?;
                if id_by_uuid(&tx, "client_notes", &note_uuid)?.is_some() {
                    report.skipped += 1;
                    continue;
                }
                let chart_id: Option<u32> = match n.chart_uuid.as_deref().map(Uuid::parse_str) {
                    Some(Ok(u)) => tx
                        .query_row(
                            "SELECT id FROM charts WHERE uuid = ?1 AND client_id = ?2",
                            params![u.to_string(), client_id],
                            |r| r.get(0),
                        )
                        .optional()?,
                    _ => None,
                };
                tx.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO client_notes (uuid, client_id, chart_id, body, created_at, updated_at)
                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)",
                    params![
                        note_uuid,
                        client_id,
                        chart_id,
                        n.body,
                        ts_or(&n.created_at, &now),
                        ts_or(&n.updated_at, &now)
                    ],
                )?;
                report.notes_added += 1;
            }
        }
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(report)
    }
}
```

`crates/store/src/csv.rs` dosyasını yalnızca test bölümüyle oluştur (2 test: `quoting_and_formula_guard`, `one_row_per_chart_without_notes`):

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::birth::fixtures::delhi;
    use crate::charts::testutil::new_client;
    use crate::db::testutil::store;
    use crate::model::{BirthData, NewClient};

    #[test]
    fn quoting_and_formula_guard() {
        assert_eq!(quote("plain"), "plain");
        assert_eq!(quote("a,b"), "\"a,b\"");
        assert_eq!(quote("say \"hi\""), "\"say \"\"hi\"\"\"");
        assert_eq!(quote("two\nlines"), "\"two\nlines\"");
        assert_eq!(text("=HYPERLINK(\"x\")"), "\"'=HYPERLINK(\"\"x\"\")\"");
        assert_eq!(text("-5"), "'-5");
        assert_eq!(text("Ayşe"), "Ayşe");
    }

    #[test]
    fn one_row_per_chart_without_notes() {
        let mut s = store();
        let (c, ch) = s
            .create_client(&NewClient {
                chart: crate::model::NewChart {
                    birth: BirthData {
                        notes: "doğum belgesi, 1. sayfa".into(),
                        ..delhi()
                    },
                    ..new_client("x").chart
                },
                ..new_client("Yılmaz, Ayşe")
            })
            .unwrap();
        s.set_chart_tags(ch.id, &["b".into(), "a".into()]).unwrap();
        s.add_note(c.id, None, "GİZLİ").unwrap();
        let csv = s.export_csv().unwrap();
        assert!(csv.starts_with("\u{feff}client_uuid,client_name,"));
        assert!(!csv.contains("GİZLİ"));
        let lines: Vec<&str> = csv.trim_end_matches("\r\n").split("\r\n").collect();
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 2);
        let expected_start = format!(
            "{},\"Yılmaz, Ayşe\",,{},,Natal,2000-01-01,17:30:00,Exact,New Delhi,India,NCT,28.6139,77.209,Iana,Asia/Kolkata,,,2000-01-01T12:00:00Z,19800,{},a; b,\"doğum belgesi, 1. sayfa\",\"{{\"\"schema_version\"\":1,",
            c.uuid,
            ch.uuid,
            atlas::tzdb_version()
        );
        assert!(lines[1].starts_with(&expected_start), "{}", lines[1]);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Uygulamayı yaz**

`crates/store/src/csv.rs` dosyasının başına, `#[cfg(test)]` satırının **üstüne** ekle:

```rust
//! CSV dışa aktarma (spec §5: yalnızca dışa aktarma). Harita başına bir satır; notlar
//! hiçbir zaman CSV'ye yazılmaz. RFC 4180: virgül ayırıcı, CRLF satır sonu, gerekirse çift
//! tırnak. Excel'in UTF-8'i tanıması için BOM ile başlar.

use rusqlite::params;

use crate::model::{ChartRecord, Client, TzChoice};
use crate::{Store, StoreError};

pub const CSV_COLUMNS: [&str; 24] = [
    "client_uuid",
    "client_name",
    "gender",
    "chart_uuid",
    "label",
    "kind",
    "date",
    "time",
    "time_certainty",
    "place_name",
    "country",
    "admin1",
    "lat",
    "lon",
    "tz_kind",
    "tz_id",
    "offset_override",
    "fold",
    "resolved_utc",
    "resolved_offset_secs",
    "tzdb_version",
    "tags",
    "birth_notes",
    "calc_settings_json",
];

/// Tek alan. Kullanıcı metni `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, sekme ya da CR ile başlıyorsa başına `'`
/// eklenir (tablolama programlarında formül enjeksiyonu). Sayı alanları bu yoldan geçmez.
fn text(s: &str) -> String {
    let guarded = match s.chars().next() {
        Some('=' | '+' | '-' | '@' | '\t' | '\r') => format!("'{s}"),
        _ => s.to_owned(),
    };
    quote(&guarded)
}

fn quote(s: &str) -> String {
    if s.contains([',', '"', '\r', '\n']) {
        format!("\"{}\"", s.replace('"', "\"\""))
    } else {
        s.to_owned()
    }
}

fn row(c: &Client, ch: &ChartRecord, calc_json: &str) -> String {
    let b = &ch.birth;
    let (tz_kind, tz_id, offset) = match &b.tz {
        TzChoice::Iana(id) => ("Iana", id.clone(), String::new()),
        TzChoice::Fixed(secs) => ("Fixed", String::new(), secs.to_string()),
        TzChoice::Lmt => ("Lmt", String::new(), String::new()),
    };
    let fields = [
        quote(&c.uuid),
        text(&c.name),
        c.gender.map(|g| g.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_owned(),
        quote(&ch.uuid),
        text(&ch.label),
        ch.kind.as_str().to_owned(),
        quote(&b.date),
        quote(b.time.as_deref().unwrap_or("")),
        b.time_certainty.as_str().to_owned(),
        text(&b.place.name),
        text(&b.place.country),
        text(&b.place.admin1),
        b.place.lat.to_string(),
        b.place.lon.to_string(),
        tz_kind.to_owned(),
        text(&tz_id),
        offset,
        b.fold.map(|f| f.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_owned(),
        text(&ch.resolved.utc),
        ch.resolved.offset_secs.to_string(),
        text(&ch.resolved.tzdb_version),
        text(&ch.tags.join("; ")),
        text(&b.notes),
        quote(calc_json),
    ];
    fields.join(",")
}

impl Store {
    /// Tüm haritalar; danışan ve harita kimliği sırasında.
    pub fn export_csv(&self) -> Result<String, StoreError> {
        let mut out = String::from("\u{feff}");
        out.push_str(&CSV_COLUMNS.join(","));
        out.push_str("\r\n");
        let mut stmt = self.conn.prepare(
            "SELECT ch.id, ch.client_id, ch.calc_settings_json FROM charts ch
             ORDER BY ch.client_id, ch.id",
        )?;
        let rows = stmt
            .query_map(params![], |r| {
                Ok((
                    r.get::<_, u32>(0)?,
                    r.get::<_, u32>(1)?,
                    r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
                ))
            })?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
        for (chart_id, client_id, calc) in rows {
            let c = self.client(client_id)?;
            let ch = self.chart(chart_id)?;
            out.push_str(&row(&c, &ch, &calc));
            out.push_str("\r\n");
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
}
```

### Task 11: `jhd.rs`: Jagannatha Hora `.jhd` içe aktarma

Belgelenmiş satır biçimi ve işaret kuralları, Latin-1 yedeği, dosya başına hata raporu ve kopya önleme (D21). İçe alınan haritalar genel varsayılanın kopyasını alır (D12).

**JHD biçimi (bu sürümün okuduğu):**

| Satır | Alan | Biçim | Örnek |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | ay | tamsayı 1–12 | `1` |
| 2 | gün | tamsayı | `1` |
| 3 | yıl | tamsayı (MS) | `2000` |
| 4 | yerel saat | `S.DDSS`, 4 haneden sonrası saniye kesri | `17.300000` = 17:30:00 |
| 5 | saat dilimi | `S.DD`, **batı pozitif** | `-5.300000` = UTC+05:30 |
| 6 | boylam | `D.DDSS`, **batı pozitif** | `-77.123240` = 77°12′32.40″ D |
| 7 | enlem | `D.DDSS`, kuzey pozitif | `28.365004` = 28°36′50.04″ K |
| 8 | yükseklik | okunmaz | |
| 9+ | diğer | sayı olmayan ilk iki boş olmayan satır: yer adı, ülke | `New Delhi`, `India` |

Şüpheli dosya: |ofset × 15° − boylam| (±180° sarmalı) > `JHD_OFFSET_LON_TOLERANCE_DEG` = 60° ise `JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch` uyarısı; dosya yine içe alınır.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/store/src/jhd.rs`
- Modify: `crates/store/src/exchange.rs` (`ImportWarningCode::JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch`)
- Modify: `crates/store/src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Store::create_client` (Task 5), `ImportReport`, `ImportWarning`, `ImportWarningCode` (Task 9), `testutil::store`, `AppPrefs`, `SettingsLoad` (testler).
- Produces (`store::jhd`):
  - Tipler ve sabitler: `JHD_OFFSET_LON_TOLERANCE_DEG`, `JhdChart`, `JhdImport`, `Packed`.
  - `pub fn decode_text(bytes: &[u8]) -> String`
  - `pub fn Packed::value(self) -> f64`
  - `pub fn parse_packed(s: &str, line: usize, field: &'static str) -> Result<Packed, StoreError>`
  - `pub fn parse_jhd(file_name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<JhdChart, StoreError>`
  - `pub fn Store::import_jhd(&mut self, files: &[(String, Vec<u8>)]) -> Result<JhdImport, StoreError>`
  - `ImportWarningCode::JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch` (yeni varyant).

`crates/store/src/jhd.rs` dosyasını yalnızca test bölümüyle oluştur (6 test: `packed_sexagesimal_values`, `delhi_file_parses_to_the_golden_moment`, `crlf_latin1_and_minimal_files`, `invalid_files_name_the_line`, `sign_mismatch_is_flagged_not_rejected`, `import_copies_defaults_reports_failures_and_skips_duplicates`):

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::db::testutil::store;
    use crate::settings::{AppPrefs, SettingsLoad};
    use jyotish_core::{AyanamsaSystem, CalcSettings};

    /// tests/golden/new-delhi-2000-01-01.json anı, JHD biçiminde (17:30 IST).
    const DELHI: &str = "1\n1\n2000\n17.300000\n-5.300000\n-77.123240\n28.365004\n0.000000\n\
                         -5.300000\n-5.300000\n0\n105\nNew Delhi\nIndia\n1\n1013.250000\n20.000000\n2\n";

    #[test]
    fn packed_sexagesimal_values() {
        let p = parse_packed("-77.123240", 6, "longitude").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            p,
            Packed {
                negative: true,
                whole: 77,
                minutes: 12,
                seconds: 32,
                nanos: 400_000_000
            }
        );
        assert!((p.value() + 77.209).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert_eq!(parse_packed("5.3", 5, "timezone").unwrap().minutes, 30);
        assert_eq!(parse_packed("+0", 5, "timezone").unwrap().value(), 0.0);
        for bad in ["", "-", "1.7000", "1.0060", "a.1", "1.2x", "--1"] {
            assert!(
                matches!(
                    parse_packed(bad, 4, "time"),
                    Err(StoreError::Jhd {
                        line: 4,
                        field: "time"
                    })
                ),
                "{bad:?}"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn delhi_file_parses_to_the_golden_moment() {
        let c = parse_jhd("Test Kişi.jhd", DELHI.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.name, "Test Kişi");
        assert!(c.warnings.is_empty());
        let b = &c.birth;
        assert_eq!(
            (b.date.as_str(), b.time.as_deref()),
            ("2000-01-01", Some("17:30:00"))
        );
        assert_eq!(b.tz, TzChoice::Fixed(19_800));
        assert!((b.place.lon - 77.209).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!((b.place.lat - 28.6139).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert_eq!(
            (b.place.name.as_str(), b.place.country.as_str()),
            ("New Delhi", "India")
        );
        assert_eq!(b.notes, "JHD: Test Kişi.jhd");
        let (_, r) = crate::birth::resolve_birth(b).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.jd_ut, 2_451_545.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn crlf_latin1_and_minimal_files() {
        let mut bytes =
            b"7\r\n20\r\n1969\r\n20.174000\r\n5.000000\r\n74.045100\r\n4.383500\r\n0\r\n".to_vec();
        bytes.extend_from_slice(b"Bogot\xe1\r\nColombia\r\n");
        let c = parse_jhd("a.jhd", &bytes).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.birth.place.name, "Bogotá");
        assert_eq!(c.birth.tz, TzChoice::Fixed(-18_000));
        // 74°04′51″ B.
        assert!((c.birth.place.lon + 74.080_833_333).abs() < 1e-6);
        assert!(c.warnings.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(c.birth.time.as_deref(), Some("20:17:40"));
        // Yalnızca 7 satır: yer adı boş.
        let c = parse_jhd("b.jhd", b"1\n1\n2000\n12.0\n0\n0\n51.3000").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.birth.place.name, "");
        assert_eq!(c.birth.place.lat, 51.5);
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_files_name_the_line() {
        let cases: [(&str, usize, &str); 7] = [
            ("1\n1\n2000\n", 4, "missing"),
            ("13\n1\n2000\n12\n0\n0\n0", 1, "month"),
            ("2\n30\n2000\n12\n0\n0\n0", 2, "day"),
            ("1\n1\n2000\n24.0\n0\n0\n0", 4, "time"),
            ("1\n1\n2000\n12\n-19\n0\n0", 5, "timezone"),
            ("1\n1\n2000\n12\n0\n-181\n0", 6, "longitude"),
            ("1\n1\n2000\n12\n0\n0\n91", 7, "latitude"),
        ];
        for (text, line, field) in cases {
            match parse_jhd("x.jhd", text.as_bytes()) {
                Err(StoreError::Jhd { line: l, field: f }) => {
                    assert_eq!((l, f), (line, field), "{text:?}")
                }
                other => panic!("{text:?}: {other:?}"),
            }
        }
        assert!(matches!(
            parse_jhd("  .jhd", DELHI.as_bytes()),
            Err(StoreError::Jhd {
                line: 0,
                field: "name"
            })
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn sign_mismatch_is_flagged_not_rejected() {
        // Boylam işareti ters (doğu pozitif yazılmış): Delhi Atlantik'e düşer.
        let flipped = DELHI.replace("-77.123240", "77.123240");
        let c = parse_jhd("x.jhd", flipped.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.warnings, [ImportWarningCode::JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch]);
        // Kaşgar (UTC+8, 75°59′ D): fark ≈ 44°, uyarı yok.
        let kashgar = "1\n1\n2000\n12\n-8\n-75.5900\n39.2800\n";
        assert!(parse_jhd("k.jhd", kashgar.as_bytes())
            .unwrap()
            .warnings
            .is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn import_copies_defaults_reports_failures_and_skips_duplicates() {
        let mut s = store();
        let raman = CalcSettings {
            ayanamsa: AyanamsaSystem::Raman,
            ..CalcSettings::default()
        };
        s.set_app_prefs(&AppPrefs {
            calc_settings: raman.clone(),
            ..AppPrefs::default()
        })
        .unwrap();
        let flipped = DELHI.replace("-77.123240", "77.123240");
        let files = vec![
            ("Ayşe.jhd".to_owned(), DELHI.as_bytes().to_vec()),
            ("Bozuk.jhd".to_owned(), b"1\n1\n".to_vec()),
            ("Ters.jhd".to_owned(), flipped.into_bytes()),
        ];
        let r = s.import_jhd(&files).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            (
                r.report.clients_added,
                r.report.charts_added,
                r.report.skipped
            ),
            (2, 2, 0)
        );
        assert_eq!(r.failed.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(r.failed[0].0, "Bozuk.jhd");
        assert!(matches!(
            r.failed[0].1,
            StoreError::Jhd {
                line: 3,
                field: "missing"
            }
        ));
        assert_eq!(
            r.report.warnings,
            [ImportWarning {
                item: "Ters.jhd".into(),
                code: ImportWarningCode::JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch
            }]
        );
        let hits = s.search_clients("ayse", 10).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(hits.len(), 1);
        let ch = s.chart(hits[0].client.last_chart_id.unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ch.resolved.jd_ut, 2_451_545.0);
        assert_eq!(
            s.calc_settings(ch.id).unwrap(),
            SettingsLoad::Current(raman)
        );
        let again = s.import_jhd(&files[..1]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((again.report.clients_added, again.report.skipped), (0, 1));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Uygulamayı yaz**

`crates/store/src/jhd.rs` dosyasının başına, `#[cfg(test)]` satırının **üstüne** ekle:

```rust
//! Jagannatha Hora `.jhd` içe aktarma (spec §5: yalnızca içe aktarma; içe alınan haritalar
//! o anki genel varsayılanların kopyasını alır).
//!
//! Biçim: düz metin, satır başına bir değer. Kişinin adı dosya adıdır. Bu sürümün okuduğu
//! satırlar (1'den sayılır):
//!
//! | Satır | Alan | Biçim |
//! |---|---|---|
//! | 1 | ay | tamsayı 1–12 |
//! | 2 | gün | tamsayı |
//! | 3 | yıl | tamsayı (MS) |
//! | 4 | yerel saat | `S.DDSS` (17.300000 = 17:30:00); 4 haneden sonrası saniye kesri |
//! | 5 | saat dilimi | `S.DD`, **batı pozitif** (−5.300000 = UTC+05:30) |
//! | 6 | boylam | `D.DDSS`, **batı pozitif** (−77.123240 = 77°12′32.40″ D) |
//! | 7 | enlem | `D.DDSS`, kuzey pozitif |
//! | 8 | yükseklik | okunmaz |
//! | 9+ | diğer | sayı olmayan ilk iki boş olmayan satır yer adı ve ülke; diğerleri okunmaz |
//!
//! İşaret kuralları gerçek dosyalarla doğrulanacaktır (spec §5); şüpheli dosyada
//! `JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch` uyarısı verilir.

use std::path::Path;

use atlas::{Date, Time};
use rusqlite::{params, OptionalExtension};

use crate::exchange::{ImportReport, ImportWarning, ImportWarningCode};
use crate::model::{BirthData, ChartKind, NewChart, NewClient, Place, TimeCertainty, TzChoice};
use crate::{Store, StoreError};

/// Ofset × 15° ile boylam arasındaki en büyük kabul edilen fark (derece). Batı Çin
/// (Kaşgar, UTC+8, 76° D) ≈ 44°; tek işaret hatası Hindistan'da ≈ 160° verir.
pub const JHD_OFFSET_LON_TOLERANCE_DEG: f64 = 60.0;

/// Ayrıştırılmış JHD dosyası.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct JhdChart {
    pub name: String,
    pub birth: BirthData,
    pub warnings: Vec<ImportWarningCode>,
}

/// Dosya kümesi sonucu: başarılı olanlar `report`'ta, okunamayanlar dosya adıyla `failed`'da.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct JhdImport {
    pub report: ImportReport,
    pub failed: Vec<(String, StoreError)>,
}

/// UTF-8 değilse Latin-1 (Windows'ta yazılmış eski dosyalar); baştaki BOM atılır.
pub fn decode_text(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    match std::str::from_utf8(bytes) {
        Ok(s) => s.trim_start_matches('\u{feff}').to_owned(),
        Err(_) => bytes.iter().map(|&b| char::from(b)).collect(),
    }
}

/// `[-]B.DDSS[kesir]` paketli altmışlık değer → (işaret, bütün, dakika, saniye, nanosaniye).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Packed {
    pub negative: bool,
    pub whole: u32,
    pub minutes: u32,
    pub seconds: u32,
    pub nanos: u32,
}

impl Packed {
    /// Bütün birim cinsinden işaretli değer (derece ya da saat).
    pub fn value(self) -> f64 {
        let v = f64::from(self.whole)
            + f64::from(self.minutes) / 60.0
            + (f64::from(self.seconds) + f64::from(self.nanos) / 1e9) / 3600.0;
        if self.negative {
            -v
        } else {
            v
        }
    }
}

pub fn parse_packed(s: &str, line: usize, field: &'static str) -> Result<Packed, StoreError> {
    let err = || StoreError::Jhd { line, field };
    let s = s.trim();
    let (negative, body) = match s.strip_prefix('-') {
        Some(rest) => (true, rest),
        None => (false, s.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(s)),
    };
    let (int, frac) = body.split_once('.').unwrap_or((body, ""));
    if int.is_empty()
        || !int.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
        || !frac.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
    {
        return Err(err());
    }
    let whole: u32 = int.parse().map_err(|_| err())?;
    let padded = format!("{frac:0<4}");
    let minutes: u32 = padded[0..2].parse().map_err(|_| err())?;
    let seconds: u32 = padded[2..4].parse().map_err(|_| err())?;
    let rest = &padded[4..];
    let nanos: u32 = if rest.is_empty() {
        0
    } else {
        format!("{:0<9}", &rest[..rest.len().min(9)])
            .parse()
            .map_err(|_| err())?
    };
    if minutes >= 60 || seconds >= 60 {
        return Err(err());
    }
    Ok(Packed {
        negative,
        whole,
        minutes,
        seconds,
        nanos,
    })
}

fn int_line<T: std::str::FromStr>(
    lines: &[&str],
    line: usize,
    field: &'static str,
) -> Result<T, StoreError> {
    lines[line - 1]
        .trim()
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| StoreError::Jhd { line, field })
}

/// `file_name` yalnızca ad için kullanılır (uzantısız kök).
pub fn parse_jhd(file_name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<JhdChart, StoreError> {
    let name = Path::new(file_name)
        .file_stem()
        .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy().trim().to_owned())
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
        .ok_or(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: 0,
            field: "name",
        })?;
    let text = decode_text(bytes);
    let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
    if lines.len() < 7 {
        return Err(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: lines.len() + 1,
            field: "missing",
        });
    }
    let month: i8 = int_line(&lines, 1, "month")?;
    let day: i8 = int_line(&lines, 2, "day")?;
    let year: i16 = int_line(&lines, 3, "year")?;
    if !(1..=12).contains(&month) {
        return Err(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: 1,
            field: "month",
        });
    }
    let date = Date::new(year, month, day).map_err(|_| StoreError::Jhd {
        line: 2,
        field: "day",
    })?;

    let t = parse_packed(lines[3], 4, "time")?;
    if t.negative || t.whole >= 24 {
        return Err(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: 4,
            field: "time",
        });
    }
    // Aralıklar yukarıda denetlendi; dönüşümler kayıpsızdır.
    let time = Time::new(
        t.whole as i8,
        t.minutes as i8,
        t.seconds as i8,
        t.nanos as i32,
    )
    .map_err(|_| StoreError::Jhd {
        line: 4,
        field: "time",
    })?;

    let tz = parse_packed(lines[4], 5, "timezone")?;
    let tz_hours_west = tz.value();
    if tz_hours_west.abs() > 18.0 {
        return Err(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: 5,
            field: "timezone",
        });
    }
    let offset_secs = -((tz_hours_west * 3600.0).round() as i32);

    let lon = -parse_packed(lines[5], 6, "longitude")?.value();
    if !(-180.0..=180.0).contains(&lon) {
        return Err(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: 6,
            field: "longitude",
        });
    }
    let lat = parse_packed(lines[6], 7, "latitude")?.value();
    if !(-90.0..=90.0).contains(&lat) {
        return Err(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: 7,
            field: "latitude",
        });
    }

    let mut texts = lines
        .iter()
        .skip(8)
        .map(|l| l.trim())
        .filter(|l| !l.is_empty() && l.parse::<f64>().is_err());
    let place_name = texts.next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
    let country = texts.next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();

    let mut warnings = Vec::new();
    let diff = (f64::from(offset_secs) / 240.0 - lon + 540.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0;
    if diff.abs() > JHD_OFFSET_LON_TOLERANCE_DEG {
        warnings.push(ImportWarningCode::JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch);
    }

    Ok(JhdChart {
        name,
        birth: BirthData {
            date: date.to_string(),
            time: Some(time.to_string()),
            time_certainty: TimeCertainty::Exact,
            place: Place {
                name: place_name,
                country,
                admin1: String::new(),
                lat,
                lon,
                geoname_id: None,
            },
            tz: TzChoice::Fixed(offset_secs),
            fold: None,
            notes: format!("JHD: {file_name}"),
        },
        warnings,
    })
}

/// Girdi kaynaklı hata mı (dosya başına raporlanır), yoksa veritabanı hatası mı (durdurur)?
fn is_input_error(e: &StoreError) -> bool {
    matches!(
        e,
        StoreError::InvalidInput(_) | StoreError::Moment(_) | StoreError::Jhd { .. }
    )
}

impl Store {
    /// Her dosya bağımsızdır. Aynı ad, tarih, saat ve koordinatta harita varsa dosya
    /// atlanır (`skipped`); böylece aynı klasörü ikinci kez içe almak kopya üretmez.
    pub fn import_jhd(&mut self, files: &[(String, Vec<u8>)]) -> Result<JhdImport, StoreError> {
        let mut out = JhdImport {
            report: ImportReport::default(),
            failed: Vec::new(),
        };
        for (file_name, bytes) in files {
            let parsed = match parse_jhd(file_name, bytes) {
                Ok(p) => p,
                Err(e) => {
                    out.failed.push((file_name.clone(), e));
                    continue;
                }
            };
            let b = &parsed.birth;
            let exists: Option<u32> = self
                .conn
                .query_row(
                    "SELECT ch.id FROM charts ch JOIN clients c ON c.id = ch.client_id
                     WHERE c.name = ?1 AND ch.date = ?2 AND ch.time IS ?3
                       AND ch.lat = ?4 AND ch.lon = ?5",
                    params![parsed.name, b.date, b.time, b.place.lat, b.place.lon],
                    |r| r.get(0),
                )
                .optional()?;
            if exists.is_some() {
                out.report.skipped += 1;
                continue;
            }
            let input = NewClient {
                name: parsed.name.clone(),
                gender: None,
                chart: NewChart {
                    label: String::new(),
                    kind: ChartKind::Natal,
                    birth: parsed.birth.clone(),
                },
            };
            match self.create_client(&input) {
                Ok(_) => {
                    out.report.clients_added += 1;
                    out.report.charts_added += 1;
                    out.report
                        .warnings
                        .extend(parsed.warnings.iter().map(|&code| ImportWarning {
                            item: file_name.clone(),
                            code,
                        }));
                }
                Err(e) if is_input_error(&e) => out.failed.push((file_name.clone(), e)),
                Err(e) => return Err(e),
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
}
```

`crates/store/src/exchange.rs` içinde `pub enum ImportWarningCode` gövdesinde:

```rust
    /// Haritanın ayarı okunamadı; o anki genel varsayılan yazıldı.
    CalcSettingsReset,
```

şununla değiştir:

```rust
    /// Haritanın ayarı okunamadı; o anki genel varsayılan yazıldı.
    CalcSettingsReset,
    /// JHD: saat dilimi ofseti ile boylam 60°'den fazla uyuşmuyor; işaret kuralı şüpheli.
    JhdOffsetLongitudeMismatch,
```

`app/src-tauri/tests/open_client.rs` dosyasını oluştur (`open_client_round_trips_and_is_fast`: oluştur → Güneş golden değerine ≤ 2″ → Raman ayarı ve yeniden hesap → görünüm, etiket, not → olay haritası → yeniden aç → dosyayı kapat ve yeniden aç → `restore_session` → DST boşluğu hatası → `not_found` → 15 açılışın ortancası ≤ 50 ms):

```rust
//! `open_client` uçtan uca: gerçek `Engine` ve dosyadaki store. Spec §8 "Danışan açma":
//! gidiş-dönüş (kaydet → kapat → aç: ayarlar, düzen, dasha yolu ve notlar birebir) ve
//! performans (Rust tarafında ≤ 50 ms). Tek test: süreçte yalnızca bir SE örneği açılabilir.

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use ephemeris::Engine;
use jyotish_core::{AyanamsaSystem, CalcSettings, Graha};
use jyotish_desk_lib::client_commands::{
    add_chart_blocking, create_client_blocking, open_client_blocking, restore_session_blocking,
    save_calc_settings_blocking, update_chart_blocking, DB_FILE_NAME,
};
use serde_json::{json, Map};
use store::{
    BirthData, ChartKind, Layout, NewChart, NewClient, Panel, PanelLayout, Place, Store,
    TimeCertainty, TzChoice, ViewState,
};

/// tests/golden/new-delhi-2000-01-01.json: 17:30 IST = JD 2451545.0.
fn delhi() -> BirthData {
    BirthData {
        date: "2000-01-01".into(),
        time: Some("17:30".into()),
        time_certainty: TimeCertainty::Exact,
        place: Place {
            name: "New Delhi".into(),
            country: "India".into(),
            admin1: "NCT".into(),
            lat: 28.6139,
            lon: 77.2090,
            geoname_id: Some(1_261_481),
        },
        tz: TzChoice::Iana("Asia/Kolkata".into()),
        fold: None,
        notes: String::new(),
    }
}

fn layout() -> PanelLayout {
    let mut settings = Map::new();
    settings.insert("varga".into(), json!("D9"));
    settings.insert("style".into(), json!("south"));
    PanelLayout {
        active_layout_id: "user:1".into(),
        layouts: vec![Layout {
            id: "user:1".into(),
            name: "Seans".into(),
            builtin: false,
            panels: vec![Panel {
                id: "d9".into(),
                kind: "chart".into(),
                x: 0,
                y: 0,
                w: 6,
                h: 12,
                settings,
            }],
        }],
    }
}

#[test]
fn open_client_round_trips_and_is_fast() {
    let ephe = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../data/ephe");
    let engine = Engine::spawn(ephe).expect("spawn engine");
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("tempdir");
    let path = dir.path().join(DB_FILE_NAME);
    let store = Mutex::new(Store::open(&path).expect("open store"));

    let created = create_client_blocking(
        &engine,
        &store,
        NewClient {
            name: "Ayşe".into(),
            gender: None,
            chart: NewChart {
                label: String::new(),
                kind: ChartKind::Natal,
                birth: delhi(),
            },
        },
    )
    .expect("create");
    assert_eq!(created.chart.jd_ut, 2_451_545.0);
    let sun = &created.chart.grahas[0];
    assert!(
        (sun.lon - 256.515_696_0).abs() * 3600.0 <= 2.0,
        "sun {}",
        sun.lon
    );
    assert_eq!(created.calc_settings, CalcSettings::default());
    let (client_id, chart_id) = (created.client.id, created.chart_record.id);

    // Ayar değişikliği yeniden hesaplanır ve kalıcıdır.
    let raman = CalcSettings {
        ayanamsa: AyanamsaSystem::Raman,
        ..CalcSettings::default()
    };
    let recomputed = save_calc_settings_blocking(&engine, &store, chart_id, raman.clone())
        .expect("save settings");
    assert_eq!(recomputed.meta.settings, raman);
    assert!(recomputed.ayanamsa_deg < created.chart.ayanamsa_deg);

    let vs = ViewState {
        panel_layout: Some(layout()),
        dasha_expanded_path: vec![Graha::Rahu, Graha::Jupiter],
        ..ViewState::default()
    };
    {
        let mut s = store.lock().unwrap();
        s.save_view_state(chart_id, &vs).unwrap();
        s.set_chart_tags(chart_id, &["araştırma".into()]).unwrap();
        s.add_note(client_id, Some(chart_id), "ilk seans").unwrap();
    }
    let event = add_chart_blocking(
        &engine,
        &store,
        client_id,
        NewChart {
            label: "Evlilik".into(),
            kind: ChartKind::Event,
            birth: BirthData {
                date: "2016-06-01".into(),
                ..delhi()
            },
        },
    )
    .expect("add chart");
    assert_eq!(event.chart_record.kind, ChartKind::Event);
    // Doğum haritasına dönüş: son açılan o olur.
    let before = open_client_blocking(&engine, &store, client_id, Some(chart_id)).expect("reopen");

    // Kapat ve yeniden aç (uygulama yeniden başlatma).
    drop(store);
    let store = Mutex::new(Store::open(&path).expect("reopen store"));
    let after = restore_session_blocking(&engine, &store)
        .expect("restore")
        .expect("a session");
    assert_eq!(after.client.id, client_id);
    assert_eq!(after.chart_record.id, chart_id);
    assert_eq!(after.calc_settings, raman);
    assert_eq!(after.view_state, vs);
    assert_eq!(after.chart_record.tags, ["araştırma"]);
    assert_eq!(after.chart_record.birth, before.chart_record.birth);
    assert_eq!(after.recent_notes, before.recent_notes);
    assert_eq!(after.recent_notes[0].body, "ilk seans");
    assert_eq!(after.chart, before.chart);
    assert!(after.warnings.is_empty());

    // Düzenle kipi: DST boşluğu kaydı engeller, harita değişmez.
    let gap = update_chart_blocking(
        &engine,
        &store,
        chart_id,
        NewChart {
            label: String::new(),
            kind: ChartKind::Natal,
            birth: BirthData {
                date: "2014-03-31".into(),
                time: Some("03:30".into()),
                tz: TzChoice::Iana("Europe/Istanbul".into()),
                ..delhi()
            },
        },
    )
    .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(gap.code, "nonexistent_local_time");
    assert_eq!(gap.params["suggestion"], "2014-03-31T04:30:00");
    let err = open_client_blocking(&engine, &store, 999, None).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(
        (err.code.as_str(), err.params["what"].as_str()),
        ("not_found", "client")
    );

    // Performans: spec §4 "Rust tarafında ≤ 50 ms". Ortanca, ısınmadan sonra.
    open_client_blocking(&engine, &store, client_id, None).expect("warm-up");
    let mut times: Vec<Duration> = (0..15)
        .map(|_| {
            let t = Instant::now();
            open_client_blocking(&engine, &store, client_id, None).expect("open");
            t.elapsed()
        })
        .collect();
    times.sort();
    let median = times[times.len() / 2];
    eprintln!(
        "open_client median {median:?}, max {:?}",
        times[times.len() - 1]
    );
    assert!(
        median <= Duration::from_millis(50),
        "open_client median {median:?}"
    );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Komutları yaz**

`app/src-tauri/src/client_commands.rs` dosyasını oluştur (birim testi `store_error_codes_are_stable` dahil):

```rust
//! Danışan veritabanı komutları (spec §4 "Danışan açma", M4). İnce katman: kayıt işleri
//! `store`'da, harita hesabı `jyotish-core`'da; burada yalnızca birleştirme ve hata eşleme.

use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use atlas::MomentError;
use ephemeris::Engine;
use jyotish_core::{CalcSettings, Chart};
use serde::Serialize;
use specta::Type;
use store::{
    ChartRecord, Client, NewChart, NewClient, Note, Store, StoreError, StoreWarning, ViewState,
};
use tauri::State;

use crate::commands::{compute_chart_blocking, CommandError};

/// Tauri durumunda tek store örneği.
pub type StoreState = Arc<Mutex<Store>>;

/// Veritabanı dosyasının adı (uygulama veri dizininde).
pub const DB_FILE_NAME: &str = "jyotish-desk.sqlite";

/// `open_client` dönüşü (spec §4: `{client, birth, calc_settings, view_state, chart,
/// recent_notes}`; doğum verisi `chart_record.birth`'tedir).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Type)]
pub struct OpenedClient {
    pub client: Client,
    pub chart_record: ChartRecord,
    pub calc_settings: CalcSettings,
    pub view_state: ViewState,
    pub chart: Chart,
    pub recent_notes: Vec<Note>,
    pub warnings: Vec<StoreWarning>,
}

impl From<StoreError> for CommandError {
    fn from(e: StoreError) -> Self {
        match e {
            StoreError::Sqlite(e) => Self::new("store_sqlite", &[("detail", e.to_string())]),
            StoreError::Migration(e) => Self::new("store_migration", &[("detail", e.to_string())]),
            StoreError::Json(e) => Self::new("store_json", &[("detail", e.to_string())]),
            StoreError::DatabaseTooNew { found, supported } => Self::new(
                "store_too_new",
                &[
                    ("found", found.to_string()),
                    ("supported", supported.to_string()),
                ],
            ),
            StoreError::NotFound { what, id } => Self::new(
                "not_found",
                &[("what", what.to_owned()), ("id", id.to_string())],
            ),
            StoreError::InvalidInput(field) => {
                Self::new("invalid_input", &[("field", field.to_owned())])
            }
            StoreError::Moment(m) => match m {
                MomentError::UnknownTimeZone(tz) => Self::new("unknown_time_zone", &[("tz", tz)]),
                MomentError::NonexistentLocalTime { local, suggestion } => Self::new(
                    "nonexistent_local_time",
                    &[
                        ("local", local.to_string()),
                        ("suggestion", suggestion.to_string()),
                    ],
                ),
                MomentError::AmbiguousLocalTime {
                    local,
                    earlier_offset_secs,
                    later_offset_secs,
                } => Self::new(
                    "ambiguous_local_time",
                    &[
                        ("local", local.to_string()),
                        ("earlier_offset_secs", earlier_offset_secs.to_string()),
                        ("later_offset_secs", later_offset_secs.to_string()),
                    ],
                ),
                MomentError::OffsetOutOfRange(secs) => {
                    Self::new("offset_out_of_range", &[("secs", secs.to_string())])
                }
                MomentError::LongitudeOutOfRange(lon) => {
                    Self::new("longitude_out_of_range", &[("lon", lon.to_string())])
                }
                MomentError::DateOutOfRange => Self::new("date_out_of_range", &[]),
            },
            StoreError::Settings(e) => e.into(),
            StoreError::LastChart => Self::new("last_chart", &[]),
            StoreError::ImportFormat(detail) => Self::new("import_format", &[("detail", detail)]),
            StoreError::ImportVersion { found, supported } => Self::new(
                "import_version",
                &[
                    ("found", found.to_string()),
                    ("supported", supported.to_string()),
                ],
            ),
            StoreError::ImportRecord { item, field } => Self::new(
                "import_record",
                &[("item", item), ("field", field.to_owned())],
            ),
            StoreError::Jhd { line, field } => Self::new(
                "jhd_invalid",
                &[("line", line.to_string()), ("field", field.to_owned())],
            ),
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn lock(store: &Mutex<Store>) -> Result<std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, Store>, CommandError> {
    store
        .lock()
        .map_err(|_| CommandError::new("store_unavailable", &[]))
}

/// `open_client` gövdesi: kayıt (store kilidi kısa tutulur), sonra motor iş parçacığında
/// harita. Bloklar; async bağlamdan `spawn_blocking` içinde çağrılmalıdır.
pub fn open_client_blocking(
    engine: &Engine,
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    client_id: u32,
    chart_id: Option<u32>,
) -> Result<OpenedClient, CommandError> {
    let rec = lock(store)?.open_client(client_id, chart_id)?;
    let place = &rec.chart.birth.place;
    let chart = compute_chart_blocking(
        engine,
        rec.chart.resolved.jd_ut,
        place.lat,
        place.lon,
        rec.calc_settings.clone(),
    )?;
    Ok(OpenedClient {
        client: rec.client,
        chart_record: rec.chart,
        calc_settings: rec.calc_settings,
        view_state: rec.view_state,
        chart,
        recent_notes: rec.recent_notes,
        warnings: rec.warnings,
    })
}

/// Oturum geri yükleme: en son açılan harita; hiç açılmadıysa `None`.
pub fn restore_session_blocking(
    engine: &Engine,
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
) -> Result<Option<OpenedClient>, CommandError> {
    let last = lock(store)?.last_opened()?;
    match last {
        Some((client_id, chart_id)) => {
            open_client_blocking(engine, store, client_id, Some(chart_id)).map(Some)
        }
        None => Ok(None),
    }
}

pub fn create_client_blocking(
    engine: &Engine,
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    input: NewClient,
) -> Result<OpenedClient, CommandError> {
    let (client, chart) = lock(store)?.create_client(&input)?;
    open_client_blocking(engine, store, client.id, Some(chart.id))
}

pub fn add_chart_blocking(
    engine: &Engine,
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    client_id: u32,
    chart: NewChart,
) -> Result<OpenedClient, CommandError> {
    let rec = lock(store)?.add_chart(client_id, &chart)?;
    open_client_blocking(engine, store, client_id, Some(rec.id))
}

/// "Düzenle" kipinin kaydı: an yeniden çözülür, harita yeniden hesaplanır. tzdb farkı
/// uyarısını kabul etmek için aynı doğum verisiyle çağrılır.
pub fn update_chart_blocking(
    engine: &Engine,
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    chart_id: u32,
    chart: NewChart,
) -> Result<OpenedClient, CommandError> {
    let rec = lock(store)?.update_chart(chart_id, &chart)?;
    open_client_blocking(engine, store, rec.client_id, Some(chart_id))
}

/// Ayar değişikliği (otomatik kayıt, "varsayılana döndür"): kaydeder ve yeniden hesaplar.
pub fn save_calc_settings_blocking(
    engine: &Engine,
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    chart_id: u32,
    settings: CalcSettings,
) -> Result<Chart, CommandError> {
    let rec = {
        let mut s = lock(store)?;
        s.save_calc_settings(chart_id, &settings)?;
        s.chart(chart_id)?
    };
    let place = &rec.birth.place;
    compute_chart_blocking(engine, rec.resolved.jd_ut, place.lat, place.lon, settings)
}

/// Bloklayan gövdeyi havuzda çalıştırır; `JoinError` `engine_stopped` olur.
pub(crate) async fn blocking<T: Send + 'static>(
    f: impl FnOnce() -> Result<T, CommandError> + Send + 'static,
) -> Result<T, CommandError> {
    tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(f)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| CommandError::new("engine_stopped", &[("detail", e.to_string())]))?
}

/// Yalnızca store işi yapan komutlar için: kilit, çağrı, hata eşleme.
pub(crate) async fn with_store<T: Send + 'static>(
    store: &StoreState,
    f: impl FnOnce(&mut Store) -> Result<T, StoreError> + Send + 'static,
) -> Result<T, CommandError> {
    let store = Arc::clone(store);
    blocking(move || Ok(f(&mut *lock(&store)?)?)).await
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use atlas::Date;

    #[test]
    fn store_error_codes_are_stable() {
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::NotFound {
            what: "chart",
            id: 7,
        });
        assert_eq!(e.code, "not_found");
        assert_eq!(
            (e.params["what"].as_str(), e.params["id"].as_str()),
            ("chart", "7")
        );
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::InvalidInput("birth.date"));
        assert_eq!(
            (e.code.as_str(), e.params["field"].as_str()),
            ("invalid_input", "birth.date")
        );
        let local = Date::constant(2014, 3, 31).at(3, 30, 0, 0);
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::Moment(MomentError::NonexistentLocalTime {
            local,
            suggestion: Date::constant(2014, 3, 31).at(4, 30, 0, 0),
        }));
        assert_eq!(e.code, "nonexistent_local_time");
        assert_eq!(e.params["suggestion"], "2014-03-31T04:30:00");
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::Moment(MomentError::AmbiguousLocalTime {
            local,
            earlier_offset_secs: 10_800,
            later_offset_secs: 7_200,
        }));
        assert_eq!(e.code, "ambiguous_local_time");
        assert_eq!(e.params["later_offset_secs"], "7200");
        assert_eq!(CommandError::from(StoreError::LastChart).code, "last_chart");
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::Jhd {
            line: 5,
            field: "timezone",
        });
        assert_eq!(
            (e.code.as_str(), e.params["line"].as_str()),
            ("jhd_invalid", "5")
        );
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::DatabaseTooNew {
            found: 3,
            supported: 1,
        });
        assert_eq!(
            (e.code.as_str(), e.params["found"].as_str()),
            ("store_too_new", "3")
        );
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::Settings(
            jyotish_core::CoreError::InvalidSettings("dasha_year"),
        ));
        assert_eq!(e.code, "invalid_settings");
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::ImportVersion {
            found: 2,
            supported: 1,
        });
        assert_eq!(e.code, "import_version");
        let e = CommandError::from(StoreError::ImportRecord {
            item: "u".into(),
            field: "uuid",
        });
        assert_eq!(
            (e.code.as_str(), e.params["item"].as_str()),
            ("import_record", "u")
        );
    }
}
```

`app/src-tauri/src/lib.rs` dosyasının **tamamını** şununla değiştir:

```rust
pub mod client_commands;
pub mod commands;

use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use tauri::path::BaseDirectory;
use tauri::Manager;

/// Komut listesi tek yerde: hem uygulama hem bağlama testi bunu kullanır.
pub fn specta_builder() -> tauri_specta::Builder<tauri::Wry> {
    // rc.29 varsayılanı `Throw`; arayüz `{ status, data | error }` sonucunu bekler.
    // Serde fazları kapalı: specta-serde her `Option<T>` alanında tipi
    // `X_Serialize | X_Deserialize` olarak ikiye böler. IPC tiplerimizde faz farkı yaratan
    // serde özniteliği (default, skip_serializing_if, asimetrik rename) yok; tek tip iki
    // yön için de doğrudur.
    tauri_specta::Builder::<tauri::Wry>::new()
        .error_handling(tauri_specta::ErrorHandlingMode::Result)
        .disable_serde_phases()
        .commands(tauri_specta::collect_commands![
            commands::engine_status,
            commands::planet_longitudes,
            commands::compute_chart,
            commands::compute_transits,
            commands::dasha_children,
            commands::print_window,
            client_commands::open_client,
            client_commands::restore_session,
            client_commands::create_client,
            client_commands::add_chart,
            client_commands::update_chart,
            client_commands::save_calc_settings,
            client_commands::save_view_state,
        ])
}

pub fn run() {
    let builder = specta_builder();
    tauri::Builder::default()
        .invoke_handler(builder.invoke_handler())
        .setup(move |app| {
            builder.mount_events(app);
            let ephe = app.path().resolve("ephe", BaseDirectory::Resource)?;
            // Eksik veri ölümcül: setup hatası Tauri'de "Failed to setup app" paniğine yol
            // açar (paketlenmiş uygulama sessizce kapanır). Yerel hata penceresi sonra eklenecek.
            let engine = ephemeris::Engine::spawn(ephe)?;
            app.manage(Arc::new(engine));
            // Danışan veritabanı: ~/Library/Application Support/<identifier>/. Açılamazsa
            // (ör. daha yeni bir sürümün veritabanı) setup hatası, motorla aynı yol.
            let data_dir = app.path().app_data_dir()?;
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&data_dir)?;
            let db = store::Store::open(&data_dir.join(client_commands::DB_FILE_NAME))?;
            let state: client_commands::StoreState = Arc::new(Mutex::new(db));
            app.manage(state);
            Ok(())
        })
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running Jyotish Desk");
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn file_commands_report_io_errors_per_file() {
        let store = Mutex::new(Store::open_in_memory().unwrap());
        let r = import_jhd_files_blocking(&store, &["/nonexistent/dir/a.jhd".into()]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.failed.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(r.failed[0].file, "a.jhd");
        assert_eq!(r.failed[0].error.code, "file_io");
        let e = import_json_file_blocking(&store, "/nonexistent/x.json").unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(e.code, "file_io");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Komutları yaz**

`app/src-tauri/src/store_commands.rs` dosyasının başına, `#[cfg(test)]` satırının üstüne ekle:

```rust
//! Kayıt yönetimi komutları (danışan, harita, not, etiket, tercihler) ve dosya içe/dışa
//! aktarma. Harita hesabı gerektirmez; gövdeler `store`'dadır.

use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use serde::Serialize;
use specta::Type;
use store::{
    AppPrefs, ChartRecord, Client, ExportOptions, Gender, ImportReport, Note, SearchHit, Store,
    TagCount,
};
use tauri::State;

use crate::client_commands::{blocking, lock, with_store, StoreState};
use crate::commands::CommandError;

/// JHD dosyası başına hata.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Type)]
pub struct FileFailure {
    pub file: String,
    pub error: CommandError,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Type)]
pub struct JhdImportResult {
    pub report: ImportReport,
    pub failed: Vec<FileFailure>,
}

fn io_error(path: &Path, e: std::io::Error) -> CommandError {
    CommandError::new(
        "file_io",
        &[
            ("path", path.display().to_string()),
            ("detail", e.to_string()),
        ],
    )
}

pub fn import_jhd_files_blocking(
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    paths: &[String],
) -> Result<JhdImportResult, CommandError> {
    let mut files = Vec::with_capacity(paths.len());
    let mut failed = Vec::new();
    for p in paths {
        let path = Path::new(p);
        let name = path
            .file_name()
            .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| p.clone());
        match std::fs::read(path) {
            Ok(bytes) => files.push((name, bytes)),
            Err(e) => failed.push(FileFailure {
                file: name,
                error: io_error(path, e),
            }),
        }
    }
    let out = lock(store)?.import_jhd(&files)?;
    failed.extend(out.failed.into_iter().map(|(file, e)| FileFailure {
        file,
        error: e.into(),
    }));
    Ok(JhdImportResult {
        report: out.report,
        failed,
    })
}

pub fn export_json_file_blocking(
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    path: &str,
    options: &ExportOptions,
) -> Result<(), CommandError> {
    let text = lock(store)?.export_json(options)?;
    std::fs::write(path, text).map_err(|e| io_error(Path::new(path), e))
}

pub fn export_csv_file_blocking(store: &Mutex<Store>, path: &str) -> Result<(), CommandError> {
    let text = lock(store)?.export_csv()?;
    std::fs::write(path, text).map_err(|e| io_error(Path::new(path), e))
}

pub fn import_json_file_blocking(
    store: &Mutex<Store>,
    path: &str,
) -> Result<ImportReport, CommandError> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| io_error(Path::new(path), e))?;
    Ok(lock(store)?.import_json(&text)?)
}
```

```rust
pub mod client_commands;
pub mod commands;
pub mod store_commands;

use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use tauri::path::BaseDirectory;
use tauri::Manager;

/// Komut listesi tek yerde: hem uygulama hem bağlama testi bunu kullanır.
pub fn specta_builder() -> tauri_specta::Builder<tauri::Wry> {
    // rc.29 varsayılanı `Throw`; arayüz `{ status, data | error }` sonucunu bekler.
    // Serde fazları kapalı: specta-serde her `Option<T>` alanında tipi
    // `X_Serialize | X_Deserialize` olarak ikiye böler. IPC tiplerimizde faz farkı yaratan
    // serde özniteliği (default, skip_serializing_if, asimetrik rename) yok; tek tip iki
    // yön için de doğrudur.
    tauri_specta::Builder::<tauri::Wry>::new()
        .error_handling(tauri_specta::ErrorHandlingMode::Result)
        .disable_serde_phases()
        .commands(tauri_specta::collect_commands![
            commands::engine_status,
            commands::planet_longitudes,
            commands::compute_chart,
            commands::compute_transits,
            commands::dasha_children,
            commands::print_window,
            client_commands::open_client,
            client_commands::restore_session,
            client_commands::create_client,
            client_commands::add_chart,
            client_commands::update_chart,
            client_commands::save_calc_settings,
            client_commands::save_view_state,
            store_commands::update_client,
            store_commands::delete_client,
            store_commands::delete_chart,
            store_commands::list_clients,
            store_commands::list_charts,
            store_commands::search_clients,
            store_commands::add_note,
            store_commands::update_note,
            store_commands::delete_note,
            store_commands::list_notes,
            store_commands::set_chart_tags,
            store_commands::list_tags,
            store_commands::get_app_prefs,
            store_commands::set_app_prefs,
            store_commands::export_json,
            store_commands::export_csv,
            store_commands::import_json,
            store_commands::import_jhd,
        ])
}

pub fn run() {
    let builder = specta_builder();
    tauri::Builder::default()
        .invoke_handler(builder.invoke_handler())
        .setup(move |app| {
            builder.mount_events(app);
            let ephe = app.path().resolve("ephe", BaseDirectory::Resource)?;
            // Eksik veri ölümcül: setup hatası Tauri'de "Failed to setup app" paniğine yol
            // açar (paketlenmiş uygulama sessizce kapanır). Yerel hata penceresi sonra eklenecek.
            let engine = ephemeris::Engine::spawn(ephe)?;
            app.manage(Arc::new(engine));
            // Danışan veritabanı: ~/Library/Application Support/<identifier>/. Açılamazsa
            // (ör. daha yeni bir sürümün veritabanı) setup hatası, motorla aynı yol.
            let data_dir = app.path().app_data_dir()?;
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&data_dir)?;
            let db = store::Store::open(&data_dir.join(client_commands::DB_FILE_NAME))?;
            let state: client_commands::StoreState = Arc::new(Mutex::new(db));
            app.manage(state);
            Ok(())
        })
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running Jyotish Desk");
}
```
